# Mixle Core 0.8.0 finding dispositions

- **Candidate revision:** `26d5ba86034321631fcaacad2068d3653223594a`
- **Source ledger:** `docs/audits/0.8.0-exhaustive-code-review.md`
- **Disposition date:** 2026-07-30
- **Total findings:** 1,854

This index gives every MXR finding exactly one current disposition. `Unverified` is intentionally separate from `fixed`: it means the cited surface may have changed, but the finding's own adverse contract has not been re-run or tied to a traceable repair. Unverified findings are therefore not release-closed.

| Disposition | Count | Release-closed? |
|---|---:|---|
| Fixed by recorded evidence | 471 | Yes for this finding |
| Confirmed not fixed | 74 | No |
| Unverified | 1,308 | No |
| Withdrawn auditor error | 1 | Not applicable |

The three repairs after the source ledger's exact-candidate pass are MXR-080-1838, MXR-080-1842, and MXR-080-1853. The other fourteen findings from that pass still reproduce or remain source-confirmed. No unverified finding is inferred fixed from a changed filename, a passing nearby test, or an uncited prose commit.
